No, like I said, when I'm alone at home, I don't really do anything like that. It's really just at work. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
No, I don't think so. I mean, I know who I am and where I am when it happens. I'm right there in the store, looking at my employees. It's just... the words that come out aren't right.
